    COMPANY PROFILE

    本集團是中國包裝飲用水行業和中國即飲軟                             The Group is a renowned company in China’s packaged drinking
    飲行業的知名企業 。發展至今 ，本集團已                            water and ready-to-drink soft beverage industry. To date, the Group
    成長為中國第二大包裝飲用水企業以及中國                             has grown into the second largest company in the packaged drinking
    最大的飲用純淨水企業 。以本集團的「怡寶」                           water market in China and the largest company in the purified drinking
    品牌為中樞 ，以飲用純淨水產品的成功為根                            water market in China. Anchored on its “C’estbon” (怡寶) brand, and
    基 ，本集團通過豐富水種 、拓展產品規格 ，                          rooted in the success of its purified drinking water products, the
    不斷豐富本集團的包裝飲用水產品矩陣 ，覆                            Group persistently enhances its product portfolio of packaged drinking
    蓋包括外出 、居家 、商務 、餐飲 、運動等多                         water products through diversification of water types and expansion
    種消費場景 。                                         of product specifications, catering to a wide range of consumption
                                                    scenarios including outdoor, indoor, business, catering and sports.

                                                                     主席報告
                                                       CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT

尊敬的各位股東：                              Dear Shareholders,


2025年國內經濟平穩運行 ，GDP全年增長                In 2025, China’s economy operated steadily, with GDP growing by
5.0% ，社會消費品零售總額累計同比增長                 5.0% for the year, total retail sales of consumer goods increasing by
3.7% ，國內物價走勢平穩 ，消費市場需求                3.7% year-on-year, domestic prices remaining stable, and consumer
及渠道形態更趨多元化 ，各年齡層消費者需                  market demand and channel formats becoming more diversified, with
求明顯分化 。面對年內消費市場的一系列趨                  a clear divergence in the needs of consumers across different age
勢變化 ，本集團始終堅持高質量發展導向 ，                 groups. In response to these evolving trends in the consumer market
全年持續改善公司業務發展結構 。本集團                   throughout the year, the Group has remained steadfast in pursuing
2025年收入為人民幣11,002.1百萬元 ，母公            high-quality development, and continuously optimized our business
司應佔利潤人民幣985.3百萬元 ，同時本集                structure throughout the year. In 2025, the Group’s revenue was
團運營資金狀況持續穩健 。                         RMB11,002.1 million, and profit attributable to the parent company
                                      was RMB985.3 million. Additionally, the Group’s working capital
                                      position remained robust.


本集團始終堅持「品牌引領業務增長」的發展                  With “healthy, safe and beautiful life” as the brand spirit, the Group
導 向 ， 以「 安 全 、 健 康 、 美 好 的 生 活 」為 品   has been committed to “brand-led business growth” in respect of
牌精神內涵 ，為廣大消費者提供優質產品 。                 its development, providing quality products to consumers. Aiming
本集團致力於成為水之集大成者 ，本年度持                  at becoming a top manufacturer of packaged drinking water, the
續多元水種運作 ，貼近消費場景 ，不斷豐富                 Group continued to diversify its water portfolio and tailor products to
包裝水產品規格 。同時飲料產品持續升級優                  specific consumption scenarios this year, while constantly enriching
化 ，豐富自有品牌產品矩陣 ，全方位提升全                 the specifications of its packaged water products. Meanwhile, the
鏈路管理能力 ，持續打造非水飲料的第二增                  Group has upgraded and optimized our beverage offerings, expanded
長曲線 。2025年 ，我們攜手中國國家隊開啟               its proprietary brand portfolio, and enhanced full-chain management
第二週期合作 ，並成為中超 、中甲 、中乙三                capabilities comprehensively, thereby continuously developing the
級職業聯賽的統一冠名商 ，開創了中國足球                  second growth curve for non-water beverages. In 2025, we entered
職業聯賽冠名史上的先例 。這不僅是華潤飲                  into the second phase of our partnership with TEAM CHINA, and
料在包裝飲用水及非酒精飲料領域高質量 、                  became the unified title sponsor for the three-tier Chinese professional
高標準的體現 ，也進一步夯實了「相伴 、高                 football leagues (Super League, League One, and League Two),
質量 、值得信賴」的品牌內涵 ，更以實際行                 setting a precedent in the history of title sponsorship for Chinese
動持續助力中國體育事業前行 。                       professional football leagues. This not only demonstrates CR
                                      Beverage’s commitment to high quality and high standards in the
                                      packaged water and non-alcoholic beverage sectors, but also further
                                      reinforces our brand essence of “companionship, high quality, and
                                      trustworthiness”. Moreover, it reflects our active contribution to the
                                      advancement of sports in China.

                                                                                       ANNUAL REPORT 2025
     主席報告
     CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT


     在成本與效益管理方面 ，本集團通過供應鏈                            In terms of cost efficiency, the Group has achieved significant cost

強化品牌在咖啡細分市場的佈局 。          and “Jasmine Tea” (茉莉玉露), emphasizing zero sugar, zero fat,
                          and authentic tea aroma to meet consumers’ demand for lighter
                          beverage options. Meanwhile, the classic product “Yan Bei” (焰焙咖
                          啡) was upgraded to a canned format, broadening its consumption
                          scenarios and enhancing portability and consistency, and to further
                          cover hot vending scenarios, strengthening the brand’s presence in
                          the coffee segment.

性提升產品品質與供應鏈掌控力 ，相關階段      conduct research on the mechanisms and control technologies for

     外匯風險                                              Foreign Exchange Risk

     本集團的大部分收入和大部分支出均以人民                               The majority of the Group’s revenue and expenditures are denominated
     幣計價 。本集團的附屬公司在中國大陸及香                              in Renminbi. The Group’s subsidiaries operate in Chinese Mainland and
     港營運 ，並分別以當地貨幣作為其功能貨幣                              Hong Kong, with their functional currencies being the local currencies
     （即人民幣及港元）。本集團進行若干以外幣                              (i.e., RMB and HKD), respectively. The Group engages in certain
     計值的交易 ，因此產生匯率波動風險 。本集                             transactions denominated in foreign currencies, thereby exposing it to
     團目前並無外幣對沖政策 。然而 ，管理層會                             exchange rate fluctuation risks. Currently, the Group does not have a
     監察外匯風險 ，並將於有需要時考慮對沖重                              foreign currency hedging policy in place. However, the management
     大外幣風險 。                                           actively monitors foreign exchange risks and will consider hedging
                                                       significant foreign currency exposures when necessary.

本集團堅持「以人為本」，致力於建立專業高       Adhering to the people-oriented principle, the Group is committed
效的管理體系 ，在所有僱傭實踐中提供公平       to building a highly efficient professional management system, which
和平等的機會 ，通過完善人才選拔和培養機       provides fair and equal opportunities in all employment practices,
制 ，包括為新入職員工提供企業文化 、業       and through improving talents selection and fostering mechanisms
務及行業的新員工培訓 ，以提高彼等對本集       including but not limited to providing new hire training to new joiners
團的瞭解及履行職責的能力；定期向員工提        on our corporate culture, business and industry, to improve their
供度身訂造的內部培訓課程或安排員工參加        understanding of our Group and their abilities to perform their duties.
第三方提供的培訓課程 ，以提高彼等的技術       The Group also regularly provides tailor-made in-house training
技能；為若幹員工及優秀年輕骨幹提供管理        sessions to our employees or arranges for our employees to attend
技能培訓機會 ，以幫助彼等過渡至管理職位       training sessions provided by third parties, thus improving their
等 ，鼓勵員工充分發揮創造力 ，保持核心團      technical skills; by providing management skills training opportunities
隊的積極性和穩定性 。同時 ，本集團遵循法      to certain employees and outstanding young backbones to facilitate
律法規有關工資待遇及福利的要求 ，不斷完       their transition into a management role and other means, we encourage
善薪酬福利管理辦法 ，努力為員工提供全面       employees to fully exert their creativity, maintaining the initiative and
且具有競爭力的薪酬福利保障 ，包括為員工       stability of core teams. Meanwhile, the Group complies with the
提供具競爭力的薪金 、全面的保險計劃及以       requirements of laws and regulations on salary and welfare, and
績效為基礎的獎勵計劃 ，該等計劃通常基於       continuously optimizes the Management Regulations for Remuneration
員工個人的表現及本集團業務的整體表現 。       and Benefits (薪酬福利管理辦法), striving to provide comprehensive
另外 ，我們通過多元化的文體活動豐富員工       and competitive remuneration and welfare package, including but not

大疾病 、重大意外事故的員工家庭及時發放       insurance packages and merit-based incentive schemes, which are

幣700萬元 。                   the leisure life of employees through diversified cultural and sports

止年度之企業管治報告 。              the Company for the year ended December 31, 2025.


企業文化                      CORPORATE CULTURE
本集團以「十四五」華潤企業文化理念體系為      Based on the “14th Five-Year Plan” corporate culture philosophy
基石 ，與業務目標緊密相連 ，推動企業穩      of China Resources, the Group closely adheres to its business
健前行 。本集團堅持引領行業發展 ，以「健     objectives to drive its stable advancement. The Group insists on
康 、安全 、專業」為品牌形象 ，為廣大消費    leading industry development and providing the quality products to
者提供優質產品 ，不斷深入挖掘市場增長潛      consumers with “healthy, safe and professionalism” as the brand
力 ，致力於為消費者提供更加多元化的選擇      image, and continuously explores market growth potential, striving to
與更高品質的產品 。本集團以「誠實守信 、     offer consumers more diversified choices and higher quality products.
業績導向 、以人為本 、合作共贏」為企業價     With the corporate values of “integrity, performance-orientation,
值觀 ，打造真誠 、團結 、開放 、進取的組織   people-oriented, and win-win cooperation”, the Group fosters an
氛圍 ，將企業文化融入到企業日常運營中 ，     organizational atmosphere of sincerity, solidarity, open-mindedness
成為指導員工行為的內在動力 。           and proactivity, and integrates corporate culture into its daily operations
                          to make it an intrinsic motivation that guides employees’ behaviors.


企業管治常規                    CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES
本公司深知在本集團管理架構及內部控制程       The Company is well aware of the importance of incorporating excellent
序引入優良的企業管治元素的重要性 ，藉以      corporate governance elements into the Group’s management structure
達致有效的問責性 。                and internal control process to achieve effective accountability.


本公司已應用企業管治守則所載的原則並採       The Company has applied the principles outlined in the Corporate
納當中所述的守則條文 。本公司堅信 ，董事     Governance Code and adopted the code provisions described therein.
會中執行董事 、非執行董事與獨立非執行董      The Company firmly believes that the composition of executive
事的組合應保持平衡 ，以使董事會有強大的      Directors, non-executive Directors and independent non-executive
獨立性 ，能夠有效作出獨立判斷 。         Directors of the Board should be balanced to ensure the Board
                          maintains strong independence and can effectively make independent
                          judgments.


本公司於截至2025年12月31日止年度一直遵   The Company has complied with all applicable code provisions set
守企業管治守則列載之所有適用守則條文 。      forth in the Corporate Governance Code throughout the year ended
                          December 31, 2025.

涵蓋之其他事項 。                 and other matters covered by the Model Code.

的利益 。董事會授予本集團高級管理層有關     The Board makes objective decisions to safeguard the interests of
本集團日常管理及營運的權限及職責 。       the Company and its Shareholders. The Board grants the senior
                         management of the Group the authority and duties related to the
                         daily management and operations of the Group.

股息政策                                  DIVIDEND POLICY
本公司是一家根據開曼群島法律以存續方式                   Our Company is a holding company registered by way of continuation
註冊成立的控股公司 。因此 ，任何未來股息                 under the laws of the Cayman Islands. As a result, the payment
的支付和金額將取決於能否從本集團的附屬                   and amount of any future dividend will depend on the availability
公司獲得股息 。本集團過往的股息分派記錄                  of dividends received from our Group’s subsidiaries. Our Group’s
不可作為決定本集團未來可能宣派或支付的                   dividend distribution record in the past may not be used as a reference
股息水平的參考或依據 。本集團目前並無股                  or basis to determine the level of dividends that may be declared
息政策或預設的股息支付率 。股息的分派由                  or paid by our Group in the future. Our Group currently does not
董事會根據適用的法律法規酌情決定 。宣派                  have a dividend policy or a pre-determined dividend payout ratio.
或支付任何股息的決定及其金額取決於多項                   Distribution of dividends shall be decided by our Board at their
因素 ，包括但不限於本集團的經營業績 、現                 discretion in compliance with the applicable laws and regulations. A
金流量及財務狀況 、經營及資本開支需求 、                 decision to declare or to pay any dividends and the amount thereof
本集團附屬公司的可供分派利潤及其向本公                   depends on various factors, including but not limited to our Group’s
司支付的股息 、未來計劃及業務前景 、市                  results of operations, cash flows and financial position, operating and
場狀況 、組織章程細則 、監管限制及本集團                 capital expenditure requirements, distributable profits of our Group’s
的合同責任等 。除本集團合法可供分派的利                  subsidiaries and dividends they pay to the Company, future plans and

     董事會多元化政策                                        BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY
     本集團已採納董事會多元化政策 ，當中載列                            The Group has adopted a board diversity policy which sets out the
     實現及維持董事會多元化的方法 。根據本公                            approach to achieve and maintain diversity in its Board. Pursuant to
     司的董事會多元化政策 ，董事會候選人的甄                            the Company’s board diversity policy, selection of Board candidates
     選將基於一系列多元化角度 ，包括但不限於                            will be based on a range of diversity perspectives, including but not
     性別 、年齡 、文化及教育背景 、行業經驗 、                         limited to gender, age, cultural and educational background, industry
     技術能力 、專業資格及技能 、知識 、任職年                          experience, technical capabilities, professional qualifications and skills,
     限及其他相關因素 。本集團亦會考慮自身的                            knowledge, length of service and other related factors. The Group
     業務模式及特殊需求 。董事候選人的最終甄                            will also consider its own business model and special needs. The

風險管理及內部監控                  RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL
1.    用於辨認 、評估及管理重大風險的程    1.     Procedures for identifying, assessing and managing
      序                           significant risks

      本集團每年通過「自下而上 、上下結           The Group conducts major risk assessments annually through
      合」的方式進行重大風險評估 ，通過           a “bottom-up and vertical integration” method, and promotes
      風險管理職能推動各業務單位及職能            all business units and functional departments through risk
      部門 ，結合內外部環境變化 、行業特          management functions, taking into account changes in the
      點，圍繞發展戰略和經營目標，對             internal and external environment and industry characteristics,
      價值鏈各環節關鍵風險進行識別 、評           focusing on development strategies and business objectives,
      估 ，全面識別 、預判可能面臨的重大          identifying and assessing key risks in each link of the value
      風險 ，並通過明確風險防控目標及責           chain, comprehensively identifying and predicting the major
      任主體 、加強風險趨勢研判 、動態監          risks that may be faced, and carrying out dynamic prevention
      測風險變化 、完善內控管理等方面進           and control by clarifying risk prevention and control objectives
      行動態防控 。                     and responsible entities, strengthening risk trend research and
                                  judgment, dynamically monitoring risk changes, and improving
                                  internal control management.

     主要風險                      Major risks

     在競爭激烈的市場環境中，本集團           In the market environment with intense competition, major risks
     2025年度主要風險分佈於市場和運營        of the Group in 2025 are located in the aspects of market and
     方面 ，我們根據本集團實際制定了切         operation. We had formulated feasible measures in accordance
     實可行的應對措施對該等風險進行有          with the Group’s actual situation, so as to conduct effective
     效管理 。市場風險主要包括宏觀經濟         management on such risks. Market risks mainly include risks in
     風險 、市場競爭風險 ，本集團通過定        macro-economy and market competition. The Group performs
     期分析行業趨勢 、跟進戰略執行 、增        in-time identification and effective management measures
     加新品儲備 、聚焦營銷資源等 ，對市        on market risks through regularly analyzing industry trend,
     場風險進行及時識別和有效管理 。運         tracking strategic implementation, expanding new product
     營風險主要包括產品質量風險 、存貨         reserves, focusing marketing resources and other measures.
     管理風險 、渠道管理風險 ，本集團通        Operation risks mainly include risks on product quality,
     過完善質量管理體系 、落實質量管理         inventory management, and channel management. The Group
     責任 、加強產銷協調 、提升經銷商經        manages these risks through key measures such as enhancing
     營能力 、拓展新興渠道等關鍵舉措進         the quality management system, enforcing quality control
     行風險管控 。                   accountability, strengthening production-sales coordination,
                               improving distributors’ operational capabilities, and expanding
                               into emerging channels.


2.   風險管理及內部監控系統的主要特點     2.   Major Characteristics of Risk Management and Internal
                               Monitoring System

     本集團的風險管治及內部監控架構基          The Group’s structure of risks governance and internal
     於「三道防線」模型 ，明確風險管理分        monitoring is based on the model of “three lines of defense”,
     工與管理職責 。第一道防線，由各          which specifies risk management division and management
     級業務單位構成 ，位於業務流程及相         responsibility. The first line of defense is constituted with
     伴生的風險的前端 ，側重對各項業務         business units at all levels, in the front end of business process
     相關風險進行直接的 、事前和事中的         and associated risks, and places emphasis on the direct,
     防範和控制；第二道防線 ，由風險管         prior– and during-event prevention and control over risks
     理、財務、法務及其他職能共同構           related to each business. The second line of defense is jointly
     成 ，輸出風險相關的理念 、體系和方        constituted by the risk management, finance, legal and other
     法 ，對「第一道防線」的運作進行合理        functional departments, generates risk-related ideas, systems
     的監控和指導 ，確保「第一道防線」管        and methods, and conducts reasonable monitoring and guiding
     理效果的最優化；第三道防線 ，由本         in respect of the function of the “first line of defense”, thus
     集團董事會下設的審核委員會及內部          ensuring maximum management results of the “first line of
     審計職能構成 ，對前兩道防線的運作         defense”. The third line of defense is constituted with the Audit
     進行事後檢查 、評估 ，明確並組織整        Committee under the Board and the internal audit functional
     改存在的問題 ，確保風險管理各項政         departments of the Group, performs post-event inspection
     策 、職責的落實及持續優化 。通過多        and assessment to the operation of the first two lines, defines
     層次 、全方位的風險管理與內部監控         and organizes rectification for existing problems, so as to
     體系 ，本集團能夠更加有效地識別 、        guarantee the implementation and continuous optimization
     評估 、監控和應對各種風險 ，保障長        of various policies and responsibilities of risk management.
     期穩定發展 。                   Through the multi-level and comprehensive risk management
                               and internal monitoring system, the Group is able to more
                               effectively identify, evaluate, monitor and respond to all risks,
                               safeguarding long-term and stable development.

     披露內幕消息                                          DISCLOSURE OF INSIDE INFORMATION
     本集團重視適當處理和傳閱內幕消息 。本集                            The Group attaches great importance to the appropriate handling
     團已參考證券及期貨事務監察委員會頒佈的                             and circulation of inside information. The Group has set up a
     內幕消息披露指引 ，設置有關披露內幕消息                            management framework for the disclosure of inside information by
     的管理框架 。在此管理框架下 ，本集團制定                           reference to the Guidelines on Disclosure of Inside Information issued
     了合理的涉及內幕消息保密 、存檔等內容的                            by the Securities and Futures Commission. Under this management
     管理制度 ，擬定合理的買賣公司證券的合規                            framework, the Group has established a reasonable management
     指引 ，設有合理及時地處理和披露內幕消息                            system that addresses the confidentiality and archiving of inside
     的程序 。例如下列步驟：管理制度中對內幕                            information, formulated a sound compliance guideline regarding the
     消息情形及內幕消息知情人範圍進行了合理                             dealing in securities of the Company, and put in place procedures
     規定 ，內幕消息知情人在涉及內幕消息的重                            for the reasonable and timely handling and disclosure of inside
     大事項的策劃 、研究 、決策過程中需要保                            information. For instance, the steps involved are as follows: the
     密 ，尋求專業意見或諮詢時要求專業機構履                            management system reasonably defines the circumstances under
     行保密義務 ，加強對內幕消息知情人檔案登                            which inside information arises and the scope of individuals who
     記管理 ，向公眾全面披露資料前 ，內幕消息                           have access to inside information; insiders are required to maintain
     知情人不得買賣本公司任何證券 ，內幕消息                            confidentiality during the planning, research and decision-making
     披露將根據上市規則要求適時履行內部審批                             processes concerning significant matters related to inside information;
     流程後進行公開披露 。                                     when seeking professional advice or consultation, it is mandatory
                                                     for professional institutions to adhere to confidentiality obligations;
                                                     efforts are made to enhance the registration and management system
                                                     for files of individuals who have access to inside information; prior
                                                     to the comprehensive public disclosure of any information, insiders
                                                     are prohibited from trading the Company’s securities; any inside
                                                     information will be publicly disclosed in accordance with the Listing
                                                     Rules after the necessary internal approval processes are completed.

南》、《環境監測與測量管理規定》在內的多項     internal environmental management systems, including the C’estbon

化管理體系以及量化指標評價系統對環境保       護管理指南》) and the Environmental Monitoring and Measurement
護和節能減排等專業領域進行系統化管理 ，      Management Regulations (《環境監測與測量管理規定》). In addition,
落實相關環境保護具體措施 ，不斷完善企       the Group employs CR Group’s factor management system and
業的環境治理體系 。在溫室氣體排放目標方      quantitative indicator evaluation system to systematically oversee
面 ，華潤飲料參照「世界能源基金會」發佈的     professional fields such as environmental protection, energy
《中國碳中和研究報告》和《華潤集團碳達峰      conservation and emissions reduction, and facilitates implementation of
行動方案》，制定了《華潤飲料碳達峰行動方      relevant environmental protection measures, thereby fostering ongoing
案》，明確了到2030年的雙碳目標及實現路     improvements in the corporate environmental governance framework.
徑 ，持續完善自身碳排放管理制度 ，推動改     In terms of greenhouse gas emission targets, with reference to the
善項目落地執行 ，開展綠色工廠創建工作 ，     Research Report on Carbon Neutrality in China published by the World
並結合公司的發展策略持續評估減碳目標的       Energy Foundation and the CR Group’s Carbon Peak Action Plan
內容和達峰效果 。                 (《華潤集團碳達峰行動方案》), CR Beverages has formulated the CR
                          Beverage’s Carbon Peak Action Plan (《華潤飲料碳達峰行動方案》),
                          clearly defining the dual-carbon goals and implementation pathways
                          for 2030. The Company is dedicated to the ongoing enhancement of
                          its carbon emission management system, promotes the execution of
                          improvement projects, carries out the establishment of green factories,

退休福利計劃                      RETIREMENT BENEFITS SCHEMES
本集團根據強制性公積金計劃（「強制性公積        The Group operates a defined contribution retirement benefits scheme
金計劃」）為合資格參與強制性公積金計劃的        under the Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme (the “MPF Scheme”)
僱員設立一項界定供款退休福利計劃 。強制        for employees who are eligible to participate in the MPF Scheme.
性公積金計劃的資產與本集團的資產分開 ，        The assets of the MPF Scheme are held separately from those of the
並由受託人控制的基金持有 。本集團按相關        Group, in fund under the control of trustees. The Group contributes
工資成本的若干百分比向強制性公積金計劃         a certain percentage of relevant payroll costs to the MPF Scheme,
作出供款 ，而該等供款由僱員作出 。          which contribution is matched by employees.


本公司於中國成立的附屬公司的僱員為中國         The employees of the Company’s subsidiaries established in the PRC
政府運作的國家管理退休福利計劃的成員 。        are members of a state-managed retirement benefit scheme operated
附屬公司須按工資成本的若干百分比向退休         by the PRC government. The subsidiaries are required to contribute a
福利計劃供款 ，以為福利提供資金 。本集        certain percentage of payroll costs to the retirement benefits schemes
團就退休福利計劃的唯一責任為作出特定供         to fund the benefits. The only obligation of the Group with respect
款。                          to the retirement benefit schemes is to make specific contributions.

方於履職過程中引致或蒙受的所有訴訟 、費       that they will not suffer any loss or damage arising from or incurred
用 、收費 、損失 、損害及開支而受損 。      in connection with any actions, costs, charges, losses, damages, or
                           expenses resulting from the performance of their duties.

意見                         OPINION
我們已審計第117至208頁所載的華潤飲料      We have audited the consolidated financial statements of China

                           consolidated financial statements, including material accounting policy

      INDEPENDENT AUDITOR’S REPORT


      關鍵審計事項                                                             KEY AUDIT MATTERS
      關鍵審計事項是根據我們的專業判斷 ，認                                                Key audit matters are those matters that, in our professional judgment,
      為對本期合併財務報表的審計最為重要的事                                                were of most significance in our audit of the consolidated financial
      項 。該等事項乃於我們審計整體合併財務報                                               statements of the current period. These matters were addressed in
      表及出具意見時進行處理 。我們不會對該等                                               the context of our audit of the consolidated financial statements as
      事項提供單獨的意見 。                                                        a whole, and in forming our opinion thereon, and we do not provide
                                                                         a separate opinion on these matters.

      關鍵審計事項                                                                             我們的審計如何處理關鍵審計事項
      Key audit matter                                                                   How our audit addressed the key audit matter
      銷量折扣的可變對價
      Variable consideration for sales volume rebates

      我們將銷量折扣的可變對價確認為關鍵審計事項 ，乃由於其在                                                       我們有關銷量折扣可變對價的程序包括：
      數額上對合併損益及其他綜合收益表而言屬重大 。                                                            Our procedures in relation to variable consideration for sales
      We identified variable consideration for sales volume rebates                      volume rebates included:
      as a key audit matter as it is quantitatively significant to
      the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other                             •   測試與記錄銷量及計算銷量折扣可變對價相關的人工控制運
      comprehensive income.                                                                  行的有效性；
                                                                                         •   Testing the operating effectiveness of the relevant manual
      銷量折扣根據不同輸入因素釐定 ，包括相應銷售合同中規定的                                                           controls over the recording of sales volume and the
      單一銷量閾值及單位折扣 ，以及年內實現的銷量 ，這增加了其                                                          calculation of variable consideration for sales volume
      計算出現錯誤的風險 。不同客戶的銷量閾值及單位折扣各有差                                                           rebates;
      異。
      The sales volume rebates are determined based on different                         •   在內部信息技術(「IT」)專家的參與下 ，測試IT對銷量記錄的一

      2.   應用新訂及經修訂香港財務報 2.                                 APPLICATION OF NEW AND AMENDMENTS
           告準則（「香港財務報告準則」）                                  TO HONG KONG FINANCIAL REPORTING
           （續）                                              STANDARDS (“HKFRSS”) (CONTINUED)
             香港財務報告準則第18號財務報表的                              HKFRS 18 Presentation and Disclosure in Financial
             呈列及披露                                          Statements

             香港財務報告準則第18號財務報表的                              HKFRS 18 Presentation and Disclosure in Financial Statements,
             呈列及披露 載列財務報表的呈列及披                              which sets out requirements on presentation and disclosures
             露規定 ，將取代香港會計準則第1號                              in financial statements, will replace HKAS 1 Presentation of
             財務報表的呈列 。本新訂香港財務報                              Financial Statements. This new HKFRS Accounting Standard,
             告準則會計準則在延續香港會計準則                               while carrying forward many of the requirements in HKAS 1,
             第1號中的多項規定的同時 ，引入新                              introduces new requirements to present specified categories
             規定以於損益表內呈列指定類別及界                               and defined subtotals in the statement of profit or loss;
             定的小計；在財務報表附註中披露管                               provide disclosures on management-defined performance
             理層界定的業績指標（「管理層界定的                              measures (“MPMs”) in the notes to the financial statements
             業績指標」）及改進於財務報表中將予                              and improve aggregation and disaggregation of information
             披露的合併及分類資料 。此外 ，香港                             to be disclosed in the financial statements. In addition, some
             會計準則第1號的部分段落已移至香                               HKAS 1 paragraphs have been moved to HKAS 8 and HKFRS

     合併財務報表已根據香港會計師公會                   The consolidated financial statements have been
     頒佈的香港財務報告準則會計準則編                   prepared in accordance with HKFRS Accounting
     製 。就編製合併財務報表而言 ，倘有                 Standards issued by the HKICPA. For the purpose of
     關資料合理預期會影響主要使用者作                   preparation of the consolidated financial statements,
     出之決定 ，則有關資料被視為重大 。                 information is considered material if such information
     此外，合併財務報表包括香港聯合                    is reasonably expected to influence decisions made by
     交易所有限公司證券上市規則及香港                   primary users. In addition, the consolidated financial
     《公司條例》所規定的適用披露 。                   statements include applicable disclosures required by
                                        the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the
                                        Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited and by the
                                        Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.


     本公司董事批准該等合併財務報表時                   The directors of the Company have, at the time of
     合理預期 ，本集團在可見將來有充裕                  approving the consolidated financial statements, a
     資源繼續營運 。因此彼等繼續採納持                  reasonable expectation that the Group has adequate
     續經營的會計基準來編製合併財務報                   resources to continue in operational existence for the
     表。                                 foreseeable future. Thus they continue to adopt the
                                        going concern basis of accounting in preparing the
                                        consolidated financial statements.

                    資產收購                                          Asset acquisitions

                    當本集團收購並不構成一項業                                 When the Group acquires a group of assets and
                    務的一組資產及負債時 ，本集                                liabilities that do not constitute a business, the Group
                    團識別及確認所收購個別可識                                 identifies and recognises the individual identifiable
                    別資產及所承擔負債 ，首先按                                assets acquired and liabilities assumed by allocating the
                    其於購買日的相對公允價值將                                 purchase price first to identifiable assets and liabilities
                    購買值分配至可識別資產及負                                 on the basis of their relative fair values at the date of
                    債 。有關交易並不會導致出現                                purchase. Such a transaction does not give rise to
                    商譽或議價收購收益 。                                   goodwill or bargain purchase gain.


                    業務合併                                          Business combinations

                    業務為一組綜合的活動及資                                  A business is an integrated set of activities and assets
                    產 ，包括一項投入及一項實質                                which includes an input and a substantive process
                    性流程 ，二者共同對創造產出                                that together significantly contribute to the ability to
                    的能力有重大貢獻 。所收購流                                create outputs. The acquired processes are considered
                    程若對持續產生產出的能力至                                 substantive if they are critical to the ability to continue
                    關重要（包括具備執行相關流                                 producing outputs, including an organised workforce
                    程所需技能 、知識或經驗的有                                with the necessary skills, knowledge, or experience
                    組織團隊）或對持續產生產出                                 to perform the related processes or they significantly
                    的能力有重大貢獻且屬獨特或                                 contribute to the ability to continue producing outputs
                    稀有或不花費大量成本 、努力                                and are considered unique or scarce or cannot be
                    或延誤持續生產產出的能力便                                 replaced without significant cost, effort, or delay in the
                    無法被取代 ，則被視為實質性                                ability to continue producing outputs.
                    流程 。


                    對業務的收購採用收購法核                                  Acquisitions of businesses are accounted for using the
                    算 。業務合併中轉讓的對價以                                acquisition method. The consideration transferred in a
                    公允價值計量 ，公允價值指本                                business combination is measured at fair value, which is
                    集團轉讓的資產、本集團對                                  calculated as the sum of the acquisition-date fair values
                    被收購方原擁有人發生的負債                                 of the assets transferred by the Group, liabilities incurred
                    以及本集團為換取對被收購方                                 by the Group to the former owners of the acquiree and
                    控制權而發行的股權在收購日                                 the equity interests issued by the Group in exchange
                    期的公允價值之和 。收購相關                                for control of the acquiree. Acquisition-related costs
                    成本通常在發生時於損益內確                                 are generally recognised in profit or loss as incurred.
                    認。

                                            impairment losses, if any.

                於本集團履約時由客戶                                an asset that the customer controls as the Group

                產 ，且本集團對迄今完                               the Group has an enforceable right to payment

                款權利 。

           至其現值 ，該貼現率反映目前                           current market assessments of the time value of money
           市場對資金時間值之評估以及                            and the risks specific to the asset (or a cash-generating

           生單位」））的獨有風險 。

           則 該 資 產（ 或 現 金 產 生 單 位 ）                 carrying amount of the asset (or a CGU) is reduced to
           之賬面值將撇減至其可收回金                            its recoverable amount. In allocating the impairment

           短期租賃及低價值資產租賃                      Short-term leases and leases of low-value assets

           本集團對自開始日期起計租賃                     The Group applies the short-term lease recognition
           期為十二個月或以下並且不包                     exemption to leases of land and buildings that have a
           含購買選擇權之租賃土地及樓                     lease term of 12 months or less from the commencement
           宇採用短期租賃確認豁免 。本                    date and do not contain a purchase option. It also
           集團亦就低價值資產租賃採用                     applies the recognition exemption for lease of low-
           確認豁免 。短期租賃及低價值                    value assets. Lease payments on short-term leases and
           資產租賃的租賃付款乃於租期                     leases of low-value assets are recognised as expense
           內按直線法確認為開支 。                      on a straight-line basis over the lease term.


           使用權資產                             Right-of-use assets

           使用權資產的成本包括：                       The cost of right-of-use assets includes:


           •    租賃負債的初始計量金                   •      the amount of the initial measurement of lease
                額；                                  liability;


           •    於開始日期或之前所作                   •      any lease payments made at or before the
                的任何租賃付款 ，減去                         commencement date, less any lease incentives
                所得的任何租賃獎勵；                          received;


           使用權資產按成本減任何累計                     Right-of-use assets are measured at cost, less any
           折舊及減值虧損計量 ，並就租                    accumulated depreciation and impairment losses, and
           賃負債的任何重新計量作出調                     adjusted for any remeasurement of lease liabilities.
           整。


           倘本集團合理確信在租賃期屆                     Right-of-use assets in which the Group is reasonably
           滿時取得相關租賃資產的擁有                     certain to obtain ownership of the underlying leased
           權 ，則使用權資產自開始日期                    assets at the end of the lease term are depreciated
           起至可使用年期屆滿止期間折                     from commencement date to the end of the useful
           舊 。否則 ，使用權資產按其估                   life. Otherwise, right-of-use assets are depreciated on
           計可使用年期及租期的較短者                     a straight-line basis over the shorter of its estimated
           以直線法折舊 。                          useful life and the lease term.


           本集團於合併財務狀況表內將                     The Group presents right-of-use assets as a separate
           使用權資產呈列為單獨項目 。                    line item on the consolidated statement of financial

                    於租賃開始日期 ，本集團按該                                At the commencement date of a lease, the Group

                    認及計量租賃負債 。在計算租                                present value of lease payments that are unpaid at
                    賃付款的現值時 ，倘租賃中所                                that date. In calculating the present value of lease
                    隱含的利率不易確定 ，則本集                                payments, the Group uses the incremental borrowing
                    團於租賃開始日期採用增量借                                 rate at the lease commencement date if the interest
                    貸利率 。增量借貸利率取決於                                rate implicit in the lease is not readily determinable.
                    租賃的期限、貨幣及起始日                                  The incremental borrowing rate depends on the term,
                    期 ，並基於一系列輸入參數予                                currency and start date of the lease and is determined
                    以釐定 ，包括：以政府債券利                                based on a series of inputs including: the risk-free rate

                    國家的風險調整；以債券收益                                 risk adjustment; a credit risk adjustment based on
                    率為基礎的信貸風險調整；以                                 bond yields; and an entity-specific adjustment whether
                    及特定實體的調整 ，視訂立租                                the risk profile of the entity that enters into the lease
                    賃的實體風險狀況是否與本集                                 is different to that of the Group and whether the lease

                    本集團的擔保而定 。


                    租賃付款包括固定付款（包括                                 The lease payments include fixed payments (including

                    賃獎勵 。                                         receivable.

                    息增幅及租賃付款予以調整 。                                adjusted by interest accretion and lease payments.

           所產生之匯兌差額會於產生期                    monetary items, and on the retranslation of monetary
           間在損益內確認 。                        items, are recognised in profit or loss in the period in
                                            which they arise.

           此外，就部分出售子公司而                     In addition, in relation to a partial disposal of a subsidiary
           言 ，倘並無導致本集團失去對                   that does not result in the Group losing control over
           子公司的控制權 ，則按比例分                   the subsidiary, the proportionate share of accumulated

           至非控股權益 ，且不會於損益                   interests and are not recognised in profit or loss.

                    惟在其與於其他綜合收益或直                                 except when they relate to items that are recognised

                    益內確認 。倘因業務合併之初                                in equity respectively. Where current tax or deferred

                    在評估所得稅處理的任何不確                                 In assessing any uncertainty over income tax treatments,
                    定性時 ，本集團考慮相關稅務                                the Group considers whether it is probable that the

                    體在其所得稅申報中使用或建                                 treatment used, or proposed to be use by individual
                    議使用的不確定稅務處理 。倘                                group entities in their income tax filings. If it is probable,
                    可能發生 ，即期及遞延稅項的                                the current and deferred taxes are determined
                    釐定與所得稅申報中的稅務處                                 consistently with the tax treatment in the income tax
                    理一致 。倘相關稅務機關不大                                filings. If it is not probable that the relevant taxation
                    可能接受不確定的稅務處理 ，                                authority will accept an uncertain tax treatment, the
                    則使用最可能的金額或預期價                                 effect of each uncertainty is reflected by using either

           予以確認及終止確認 。一般買                           derecognised on a settlement date basis. Regular way
           賣乃指按照市場規定或慣例在                            purchases or sales are purchases or sales of financial

           允價值計量 ，惟根據香港財務                           measured at fair value except for trade receivables

           同產生的貿易應收款項除外 。                           costs that are directly attributable to the acquisition

           實際利率法乃計算金融資產或                            The effective interest method is a method of calculating
           金融負債之攤銷成本以及分配                            the amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability
           相關期間利息收入之方法 。實                           and of allocating interest income over the relevant
           際利率乃於初始確認時將金融                            periods. The effective interest rate is the rate that exactly
           資產或金融負債於預計年期或                            discounts estimated future cash receipts and payments
           適用之較短期間內的估計未來                            (including all fees paid or received that form an integral
           收取之現金及付款（包括構成                            part of the effective interest rate, transaction costs and
           實際利率主要部分之所有已付                            other premiums or discounts) through the expected
           或已收費用 、交易成本及其他                           life of the financial asset or financial liability, or, where
           溢價或折讓）貼現至賬面淨值                            appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount
           之利率 。                                    on initial recognition.

                           源的資料顯示債務人不                                    external sources indicates that the debtor is
                           大可能悉數向債權人                                     unlikely to pay its creditors, including the Group,
                           （ 包 括 本 集 團 ）還 款 時                            in full (without taking into account any collaterals
                           （並未考慮本集團所持                                    held by the Group).
                           有 的 任 何 抵 押 品 ）， 違
                           約事件發生 。


                           不論上文為何 ，本集團                                   Irrespective of the above, the Group considers
                           認為 ，違約已於金融資                                   that default has occurred when a financial asset
                           產 逾 期 超 過 90 天 時 發                            is more than 90 days past due unless the Group
                           生，除非本集團有合                                     has reasonable and supportable information to
                           理及具支持性資料顯示                                    demonstrate that a more lagging default criterion
                           較長的違約期限更為適                                    is more appropriate.
                           用。

     遞延稅項資產                                   Deferred tax asset

     遞延稅項資產的實現主要依靠是否                          The realisability of the deferred tax asset mainly depends
     有足夠未來利潤或未來存在的臨時                          on whether sufficient future profits or taxable temporary
     稅務差異 。倘若產生的實際未來利                         differences will be available in the future. In cases where the

22.   合同負債（續）                        22.     CONTRACT LIABILITIES (CONTINUED)
      根據銷售協議 ，客戶有權根據向客戶                      Pursuant to the sales agreements, the customers are entitled
      提供的實際銷量向本集團收取銷量折                       to receive sales volume rebates from the Group based on
      扣 。於向客戶交付貨品後 ，本集團將                     the actual sales volume to the customers. After the delivery
      按月根據實際銷售量釐定應付予客戶                       of goods to the customers, the Group would determine the
      的銷量折扣 ，並將應付金額計入其他                      sales volume rebates payable to the customers based on the
      應付款項（附註21）。其後 ，本集團將                    actual sales volume on a monthly basis and the payable amount
      定期與客戶確認銷量折扣的結算方式                       is included in the other payables (note 21). Subsequently,
      （現金或存貨）。倘客戶與本集團確認                      the Group would confirm with the customers the settlement
      以存貨結算銷量折扣 ，應付客戶款項                      method of the sales volume rebates (either by cash or inventory)
      將重新分類至合同負債 。                           on a regular basis. If the customers confirm with the Group to
                                             settle the sales volume rebates by inventory, the payables to
                                             customers would be reclassified to contract liabilities.


      本集團並無原預期年期超過一年的收                       The Group has no revenue contract that has an original
      益合同 ，因此管理層根據香港財務報                      expected duration more than one year, thus management
      告準則第15號應用實際權宜方法 ，並                     applied practical expedient under HKFRS 15 and are not
      無披露分配至截至報告期末尚未達成                       disclosing the aggregate amount of the transaction price
      或部分達成的履約責任的交易價格總                       allocated to the performance obligation that are unsatisfied
      額。                                     or partially satisfied as of the end of the reporting period.

27.   退休福利計劃                           27.   RETIREMENT BENEFITS SCHEMES
      本集團根據強制性公積金計劃（「強制                      The Group operates a defined contribution retirement benefits
      性公積金計劃」）為合資格參與強制性                      scheme under the Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme (the
      公積金計劃的僱員設立一項界定供款                       “MPF Scheme”) for employees who are eligible to participate
      退休福利計劃 。強制性公積金計劃的                      in the MPF Scheme. The assets of the MPF Scheme are
      資產與本集團的資產分開 ，並由受託                      held separately from those of the Group, in fund under the
      人控制的基金持有 。本集團按相關工                      control of trustees. The Group contributes certain percent of
      資成本的若干百分比向強制性公積金                       relevant payroll costs to the MPF Scheme, which contribution
      計劃作出供款 ，而該等供款由僱員作                      is matched by employees.
      出。


      本公司於中國成立的子公司的僱員為                       The employees of the Company’s subsidiaries established in
      中國政府運作的國家管理退休福利計                       the PRC are members of a state-managed retirement benefit
      劃的成員 。子公司須按工資成本的若                      scheme operated by the PRC government. The subsidiaries
      干百分比向退休福利計劃供款 ，以為                      are required to contribute certain percentage of payroll costs to
      福利提供資金 。本集團就退休福利計                      the retirement benefits schemes to fund the benefits. The only
      劃的唯一責任為作出特定供款 。                        obligation of the Group with respect to the retirement benefit
                                             schemes is to make the specific contributions.

      31.    資本風險管理                                    31.   CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT
             本集團管理其資本 ，以確保本集團內                               The Group manages its capital to ensure the entities in the
             的實體將能夠持續經營 ，同時通過優                               Group will be able to continue as a going concern while
             化債務及權益平衡為股東帶來最大回                                maximising the return to shareholders through the optimisation
             報 。於該兩個年度 ，本集團的整體策                              of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy
             略維持不變 。                                         remains unchanged during both years.


             本集團的資本結構由淨債務組成 ，包                               The capital structure of the Group consists of net debts,
             括（ 如 適 用 ）銀 行 借 款 、 應 付 股 東 款                   which includes, where appropriate, bank borrowing, amounts
             項、現金及現金等價物以及本公司                                 due to shareholders, cash and cash equivalents and equity
             擁有人應佔權益（包括股本 、股份溢                               attributable to owners of the Company, comprising share
             價 、保留利潤及其他儲備）。                                  capital, share premium, retained profits and other reserves.


             本公司董事定期檢討資本架構 。作為                               The directors of the Company review the capital structure
             檢討的一部分 ，董事考慮資本成本及                               periodically. As part of the review, the directors consider the
             與資本相關的風險 。根據董事的建                                cost of capital and the risks associated with the capital. Based
             議 ，本集團將透過發行新股以及發行                               on recommendations of the directors, the Group will balance
             新債務或贖回現有債務平衡其整體資                                its overall capital structure through new share issues as well as
             本架構 。                                           the issue of the new debt or the redemption of existing debt.

                    (i)    貨幣風險                                    (i)   Currency risk

               括以下類別：                                          comprises the following categories:

 內部信貸評級                     描述
 Internal credit rating     Description

 低風險                        對手方違約風險低 ，且並無任何逾期金額 。
 Low risk                   The counterparty has a low risk of default and does not have any past-due amounts.
 監察名單                       債務人恆常於到期日後償還 ，但通常全數結算 。
 Watch list                 Debtor frequently repays after due dates but usually settle in full.
 呆賬                         自通過內部或外部資源獲得的信息初始確認以來 ，信貸風險已顯著增加 。
 Doubtful                   There have been significant increases in credit risk since initial recognition through information
                            developed internally or external resources.
 虧損                         有證據顯示該資產已發生信貸減值 。
 Loss                       There is evidence indicating the asset is credit-impaired.
 撇銷                         有證據顯示債務人陷入嚴重財務困難且本集團預期無實際收回款項的可能 。
 Write-off                  There is evidence indicating the debtor is in severe financial difficulty and the Group has no
                            realistic prospect of recovery.

       DEFINITIONS

      「細則」或「組織章程細則」                 指     本公司組織章程細則
      “Articles” or “Articles of          the articles of association of the Company
        Association”

      「聯繫人」                         指     具有上市規則所賦予的涵義
      “associate(s)”                      has the meaning ascribed to it under the Listing Rules

      「董事會」                         指     本公司董事會
      “Board” or “Board of                the board of Directors of our Company
        Directors”

      「合作生產夥伴」                      指     建立合作夥伴關係以有效滿足市場需求的生產工廠（包括代工廠）
      “Cooperative Manufacturing          manufacturing factories, including OEMs, that establish collaborative partnerships to
        Partners”                         effectively address market demands

      「企業管治守則」                      指     上市規則附錄C1所載的企業管治守則
      “Corporate Governance               the Corporate Governance Code set out in Appendix C1 to the Listing Rules
        Code”

      「中國」                          指     中華人民共和國 ，但就本報告而言 ，且僅作地區參考 ，除文義所指外 ，不包括香港 、澳門
                                          特別行政區及台灣地區
       “China” or “PRC”                   the People’s Republic of China, but for the purposes of this report and for geographical
                                          reference only and except where the context requires, excludes Hong Kong, the Macau
                                          Special Administrative Region, and Taiwan

      「本公司」、「華潤飲料」                  指     華潤飲料（控股）有限公司 ，一家以存續方式於開曼群島註冊成立的有限公司 ，其股份於聯
                                          交所上市（股份代號：2460）
       “Company”, “our Company”           China Resources Beverage (Holdings) Company Limited (華潤飲料(控股)有限公司), a
         or “CR Beverage”                 limited liability company registered by way of continuation in the Cayman Islands, with its
                                          shares listed on the Stock Exchange (Stock Code: 2460)

      「中國華潤」                        指     中國華潤有限公司 ，一家於中國註冊成立的有限公司 ，為受國資委監管的國有企業及本公
                                          司的最終控股公司
       “CRC”                              China Resources Company Limited, a limited liability company incorporated in China, a
                                          state-owned enterprise supervised by SASAC, and the ultimate holding company of the
                                          Company

      「華潤數科控股」                      指     華潤數科控股有限公司 ，由中國華潤（間接擁有華潤（集團）100%的股權）全資擁有
      “CR Digital Holdings”               CR Digital Holdings Co., Ltd. (華潤數科控股有限公司), a company wholly owned by CRC,
                                          which indirectly owns 100% equity interests in CR Holdings

      「華潤集團」                        指     華潤（集團）及其附屬公司
      “CR Group”                          CR Holdings and its subsidiaries

      「華潤（集團）」                      指     華潤（集團）有限公司 ，一家於香港註冊成立的有限公司 ，為中國華潤的間接全資附屬公
                                          司 ，亦為本公司的控股股東之一
       “CR Holdings”                      China Resources (Holdings) Company Limited ( 華 潤 ( 集 團 ) 有 限 公 司 ), a company
                                          incorporated in Hong Kong with limited liability, which is an indirect wholly-owned
                                          subsidiary of CRC, and one of our Company’s Controlling Shareholders

      「華潤電力」                        指     華潤電力控股有限公司 ，一家於香港註冊成立的有限公司 ，其股份於聯交所上市（股份代

       “CR Power”                         China Resources Power Holdings Company Limited (華潤電力控股有限公司), a company
